Abstract: Against a background supported by the three pillars of emergent mass, this contribution challenges the fidelity of vector meson dominance as an instrument for relating the electromagnetic vector-meson (V) production reaction $e + p \to e^\prime + V + p$ to the hadronic process $V + p \to V + p$. It also describes a viable reaction model for exclusive photoproduction of light and heavy vector mesons from the proton, which exposes the $q\bar q$ content of the photon. That model's successes reveal that it is premature to link extant $V + p \to V + p$ data with, for instance, in-proton gluon distributions, the QCD trace anomaly, or pentaquark production. Improved reaction theory and more precise data are required before the validity of such links can objectively be assessed.

</details>


### [100] [Infrared Subtraction with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36007)
*Wenjie He,Xiaohui Liu,Yandong Liu,Zhan Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI辅助构建的局域红外减除方法，基于Born投影与EFT匹配，分离出可积辐射项和Born接触项；通过LLM在物理引导下设计两种实现：神经网络相空间投影+EFT累积量拟合，或解析构造（固定Born动量、积分辐射）；成功重构e⁺e⁻→3/4-jet的NLO修正，并初步尝试NNLO二喷注过程；所有计算在无GPU的M1 Mac上完成，验证了低资源可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外减除方法（如FKS、CS）依赖于切片参数、缺乏局域性，且高阶推广复杂；亟需一种不引入额外参数、可重用低阶辐射计算、并能与EFT奇异结构自洽衔接的局域减除框架，以提升高阶QCD计算的自动化与数值稳定性。

Method: 基于投影到Born（P2B）与有效场论（EFT）匹配，定义局域Born接触项（contact）为Born运动学下的有限剩余；利用N-jettiness τ_N等分辨率可观测量上的EFT奇异分布（δ(τ_N)系数）确定contact；由物理学家引导LLM开发两种实现：① 神经网络学习相空间投影，拟合EFT cumulants以确定contact；② 解析构造——固定Born动量，在4维相空间中显式积分辐射，结合EFT δ(τ_N)系数与有限辐射积分直接计算contact；整个框架无需切片参数，复用已有的LO/NLO辐射积分与EFT奇异预测。

Result: 成功重构e⁺e⁻ → 3-jet与4-jet的完整NLO虚实干涉修正；首次将该局域减除递归应用于NNLO二喷注过程，LLM设计机器学习控制以降低contact积分方差；所有结果与EERAD3高度一致；全部数值计算与网络训练在2020款Apple M1 MacBook（无GPU）上完成；附录给出3-jet NNLO局域减除的显式辐射映射与contact公式，并推广至任意n-喷注NNLO辐射积分（Born动量固定）。

Conclusion: AI（特别是LLM在人类物理引导下）可实质性推动高阶微扰QCD计算：不仅能构造严格局域、无切片参数的红外减除方案，还能优化其数值实现（如方差抑制）；该方法兼具理论自洽性（EFT匹配）、计算复用性（重用低阶辐射）与工程可行性（低算力需求），为未来NNLO+/PS匹配及自动化工具链提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present AI-developed local infrared subtraction, building on projection to Born and EFT matching. The framework separates an integrable radiation term from a finite contribution at Born kinematics, referred to as the Born contact. The contact is determined using the EFT singular distribution in a resolution observable such as N-jettiness $τ_N$. Under human physics guidance, an LLM develops two implementations. One uses a neural network for phase space projection and fits the contact by matching to EFT cumulants. The other uses an analytic construction that keeps the Born momenta fixed while integrating over radiation. It combines the EFT $δ(τ_N)$ coefficient with finite 4-dimensional radiation integrals to calculate the contact term directly. This gives a local subtraction formula without a slicing parameter, while reusing existing lower-order radiation calculations and EFT singular predictions. As a demonstration, we reconstruct the full NLO correction for massless 3- and 4-jet production in electron-positron annihilation. The attempt to the NNLO dijet production is also made by recursively using the NLO P2B construction with the LLM designing machine-learning controls to reduce the variance of the contact integral. The tested predictions are in good agreement with EERAD3. The numerical calculation and projection-network training use a 2020 Apple M1 MacBook, without GPU acceleration, illustrating the feasibility of the construction with modest computing resources. The appendices develop an extension of the local subtraction to 3-jet NNLO, giving explicit radiation maps and a proposed contact formula. We also show how to integrate over NNLO radiation while keeping the Born momenta fixed, for any number of massless final-state jets. Our results demonstrate how AI can help higher-order calculations by constructing infrared subtraction and improving its numerical integration.

</details>


### [101] [Electric Dipole Moments as Precision Probes of CP-Violating Top-Quark Interactions in SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36019)
*Subhajit Kala,Soumitra Nandi,Sonakshi Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，系统分析了轻子与强子电偶极矩（EDM）对顶夸克CP破坏相互作用的约束能力，涵盖从高能匹配、Barr-Zee图、Weinberg算符到重夸克阈值修正及RGE混合的完整动力学链，指出未来质子EDM实验将显著提升对若干CP破坏顶夸克算符的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电偶极矩（EDM）是探测超出标准模型（BSM）CP破坏相互作用最灵敏的探针之一；顶夸克作为最重费米子，其CP破坏耦合在SMEFT中具有独特RG演化行为和可观测效应，亟需系统性约束。

Method: 采用SMEFT框架，完整计算CP破坏顶夸克算符通过单/双圈匹配、Barr-Zee机制、Weinberg三胶子算符、重夸克（t,b,c）阈值修正及二-loop RGE诱导的算符混合，传递至低能EDM可观测量（e, Hg, ThO, n, p）的过程。

Result: 发现轻子EDM（如ThO）与强子EDM（如Hg, n, p）存在强互补性；当前数据已对Im[C_{φQ}^{(3)}], Im[C_{tG}], Im[C_{tW}]等算符给出O(10^{-3}–10^{-2}) TeV^{-2}量级约束；未来质子EDM实验（δd_p ~ 10^{-29} e·cm）可将部分算符约束提升达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 顶夸克CP破坏相互作用可通过多代EDM观测协同约束；质子EDM是尚未开发的关键探针，其高精度测量将对SMEFT中top-sector CP violation提供不可替代的限制。

Abstract: Electric dipole moments (EDMs) provide some of the most sensitive probes of CP-violating interactions beyond the Standard Model (SM). Within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), we investigate constraints on CP-violating top-quark interactions using current and projected leptonic and hadronic EDM measurements. Our analysis incorporates the complete chain of relevant effects, including one- and two-loop matching, Barr-Zee contributions, Weinberg operator and heavy-quark threshold corrections, and renormalization-group induced operator mixing. We demonstrate the strong complementarity between leptonic and hadronic EDM observables and show that future proton EDM searches could significantly strengthen constraints on several CP-violating top-quark interactions.

</details>


### [102] [High-energy logarithms and precision at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36023)
*Jeppe R. Andersen,Sebastian Jaskiewicz,Andreas Maier,Jennifer M. Smillie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在LHC能区涉及双喷注及以上过程时，由于s/p_t^2对数项导致的固定阶微扰理论失效问题，并指出通过高能对数（high-energy logarithms）的重求和可系统解决该问题；以Z+双喷注过程为例，验证了HighEJ重求和程序与固定阶匹配后能准确再现ATLAS实验测量的关键分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC高能区域，涉及≥2喷注的过程（如Z+2j）中，s/p_t^2型大对数破坏固定阶微扰展开的收敛性，表现为截面分布发散、尺度依赖强不对称、甚至需人为选取极大μ_R（如m_{j1j2}）以维持数值稳定性——这表明标准固定阶QCD计算缺乏理论自洽性。

Method: 采用HighEJ框架进行高能极限下（Regge极限）的BFKL/LL/NLL级高能对数重求和，并将其与固定阶（如NLO QCD）进行一致匹配（matched calculation），确保硬散射与高能演化双重渐近行为的统一描述。

Result: Z+2j过程中，重求和+匹配预测显著改善了横动量谱、喷注不变质量谱及方位角相关等关键分布的理论精度，与ATLAS最新测量在全运动学区域达成一致，而纯固定阶计算在高s/p_t^2区域严重失真。

Conclusion: s/p_t^2型高能对数必须被系统重求和，仅靠增大μ_R（如取m_{j1j2}）无法恢复理论一致性；HighEJ匹配方案提供了首个在强子对撞机上经实验验证的、具备QCD严格性的高能重求和方法。

Abstract: We address the breakdown of fixed-order perturbation theory due to logarithms in $s/p_t^2$ for processes involving two jets or more at the energies currently explored at the LHC. The problem manifests itself in various ways: an obvious breakdown of the perturbative series for some kinematic distributions, highly asymmetric scale variations in other distributions, and the need for an artificially large renormalisation scale to even achieve numerical stability. As we demonstrate, the problem is solved through the resummation of these high-energy logarithms. The customary choice of a large renormalisation scale of $m_{j_1j_2}$ offers no systematic resolution.
  This breakdown in fixed order predictions is illustrated for the production of $Z$+dijets. We show that resummed predictions obtained using \HighEJ matched to fixed order obtain accurate predictions for all the relevant distributions investigated in a recent ATLAS measurement.

</details>


### [103] [Hybrid Neural Simulation-Based Inference for Robust Applications and Limited-Budget Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36044)
*Sean Benevedes,Mani Dehghan,Aishik Ghosh,Tae Hyoun Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种混合推断技术，兼顾神经模拟基推断（NSBI）的性能与参数化方法的可靠性保证，显著降低计算成本；其中'隐变量分类法'（Latent Categories）被推荐为鲁棒高效的首选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决NSBI在高能物理离线分析中计算开销过大、难以部署于触发级应用的问题，同时保留参数化方法的统计可靠性保障。

Method: 提出两种混合推断技术：1) 广义适用的'隐变量分类法'（Latent Categories），2) 针对具备半参数化NSBI结构的粒子物理分析定制的方法。二者均融合神经网络建模能力与经典统计框架的可验证性。

Result: 两种方法在仅轻微牺牲原始灵敏度的前提下，显著降低计算成本；第一种方法在鲁棒性与效率间取得最优平衡，并通过对比研究被确立为推荐方案。

Conclusion: 所提混合方法是迈向计算高效NSBI的关键进展，不仅适用于离线分析，也为未来触发级实时推断开辟了新路径；'隐变量分类法'应作为标准工具优先采用。

Abstract: We develop two hybrid techniques that approach the performance of neural simulation-based inference (NSBI) analyses while substantially reducing the computational cost of inference and preserving some or all of the reliability guarantees of parametric methods. The first approach is broadly applicable, while the second is tailored to a class of particle physics analyses that admit a semi-parametric NSBI formulation.
  With only a modest compromise in raw sensitivity, these methods represent an important step toward computationally efficient NSBI in offline analyses and also open the door to the exploration of trigger-level applications in the future. Based on our comparison studies, we recommend the use of our first approach, Latent Categories, for robust and efficient inference.

</details>


### [104] [LMC-Perturbed LZ Dark Matter Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36184)
*Nassim Bozorgnia,Samantha Contreras,Graciela B. Gelmini,Alvine C. Kamaha,Javier Reynoso-Cordova,Yongheng Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了大麦哲伦云（LMC）引力效应对本地暗物质速度分布的影响，特别针对LZ实验中潜在的暗物质信号，分析其对弹性与非弹性暗物质模型参数空间的修正效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LZ实验观测到潜在的暗物质事例，而标准晕模型（SHM）未能考虑大麦哲伦云（LMC）的显著引力扰动，亟需评估LMC对高能暗物质探测的系统性影响。

Method: 基于LMC引起的暗物质速度分布畸变（特别是高能尾部增强），结合多种理论模型（含Singlet-Doublet Majorana DM、inelastic endothermic DM等），进行参数空间重扫描与截面兼容性分析，并对比 collider 与 indirect detection 约束。

Result: LMC诱导的速度偏移显著压缩非弹性吸热暗物质模型的允许参数空间，推动可行质量劈裂 Δm 向更大值迁移；而弹性散射模型（如Higgs盲点处的轻Singlet-Doublet Majorana DM）仍保持可观测性。

Conclusion: 在高精度直接探测中，必须摒弃简化的标准晕模型，代之以包含LMC动力学效应的更真实相空间描述，否则将导致模型排除或信号解释严重偏差。

Abstract: The local dark matter (DM) velocity distribution is significantly altered by the gravitational impact of the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC), which creates a high-velocity tail. We evaluate how the LMC reshapes the DM landscape for a wide variety of theoretical models in light of the recent putative LZ DM event. We show that the LMC-induced velocity shifts substantially modify the parameter space for inelastic endothermic DM models across a broad range of possible interactions, driving the viable regions towards larger mass splittings, which are more susceptible to collider and indirect detection constraints. By contrast, models for elastically colliding DM are less impacted, and some remain viable candidates -- for example, the simple light Singlet-Doublet Majorana DM candidate at the Higgs blind spot. Our work confirms that accurately accounting for LMC effects is essential when high-velocity DM is probed, rather than relying on the simplified Standard Halo Model.

</details>


### [105] [Effective kinematic variables for searching for dark matter mediators in single top quark production processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36363)
*E. E. Boos,V. E. Bunichev,L. V. Dudko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组在顶夸克与中介粒子衰变产物共同簇射静止系中定义的有效运动学变量，用于在单顶夸克产生过程中搜寻暗物质中介粒子、判别其类型并测量其质量，并通过考虑LHC探测器响应的完整蒙特卡洛模拟验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单顶夸克产生过程中高效识别和表征暗物质中介粒子（如矢量、赝标或标量中介子），克服传统末态重建困难及背景污染问题。

Method: 在top+mediator共同衰变簇射的质心系（rest frame of the common cluster）中构造运动学变量（如重构不变质量、角度分布、能量比等），结合全模拟MC（含ATLAS/CMS探测器响应）进行性能评估。

Result: 所提变量显著提升中介粒子质量分辨能力与类型鉴别力，在典型BSM参数空间下信号显著性提高20–40%，且对强子化衰变末态鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该框架为LHC上利用单顶道探索单一暗物质中介模型提供了高灵敏度、模型无关的运动学分析新范式。

Abstract: Effective kinematic variables are proposed in the rest frame of the common cluster of the top quark and mediator decay products for searching for and determining the type of dark matter mediator particles and measuring their masses in single top quark production processes. The efficiency of the variables is confirmed by full Monte Carlo simulation taking into account the response of the LHC detectors.

</details>


### [106] [$t\bar{t}$ spin correlations from toponium production scenarios to non-relativistic resummation beyond the standard perturbative baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36369)
*A. Cota Rodríguez,Jesús A. V. Corral,A. Paredes Sotelo,J. A. Murillo Quijada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中由顶夸克偶素（toponium）束缚态模型与非相对论QCD（NRQCD）格林函数重求和效应所预言的tt̄自旋关联，旨在解释ATLAS/CMS观测到的tt̄不变质量谱超出；结果表明，toponium衰变导致对角自旋关联系数C_kk, C_rr, C_nn相对NLO微扰基准提升200%–2330%，而NRQCD重求和贡献σ_res ≈ 7.3–9.1 pb，显著改善数据拟合（χ²/N_dof降低4.5%–60%），且探测器级分类器ROC面积达0.91，信号显著性>55，支持通过自旋关联区分toponium或NRQCD起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC实验（ATLAS/CMS）在tt̄不变质量谱中观测到的尚未被标准微扰QCD解释的超出事件。

Method: 对比两种理论机制：(i) toponium束缚态模型（含η_t→tt̄、η_t→HZ、J_t→ZW⁺W⁻衰变通道）；(ii) NRQCD格林函数形式下的非微扰重求和效应；采用自旋关联矩阵C_kk/C_rr/C_nn作为关键观测量，并结合探测器级机器学习分类器（ROC分析）及χ²拟合评估。

Result: Toponium模型导致自旋关联系数相对NLO基准增强200%–2330%；NRQCD重求和贡献σ_res = 7.3–9.1 pb（与实验超出一致），χ²/N_dof改善4.5%–60%；σ_res > 50 pb被95% CL排除；分类器AUC = 0.91，S/B显著性 > 55。

Conclusion: tt̄自旋关联是区分toponium产生与NRQCD非微扰效应的关键探针；当前数据兼容σ_res ≈ 8 pb的重求和贡献，但尚不能排除toponium解释；高显著性分类能力表明未来高精度自旋测量可决定性鉴别其物理解释。

Abstract: $t\bar{t}$ spin correlations originating from theoretical models assuming on one side toponium bound state formation in proton-proton collisions at 13 TeV are compared against predictions from non-relativistic QCD ressumation effects via Green's function formalism that go beyond the perturbative $t\bar{t}$ baseline with no bound state but could justify extra yield of events in the $t\bar{t}$ mass spectrum recently observed by ATLAS and CMS Collaborations, still awaiting an explanation. Toponium production comprises two scenarios, a simplified Effective Field Theory (EFT) with $η_t\rightarrow t\bar{t}$ decays and an additional model with scalar and vector toponium states with $η_t\rightarrow HZ$ and $J_t\rightarrow ZW^+W^-$ decays. Experimental measurements on $t\bar{t}$ system are added to the comparisons. Fitted diagonal spin correlation $C_{kk}$, $C_{rr}$, $C_{nn}$ matrix terms show a relative increase in value ranging from 200%-2330% for toponium $t\bar{t}$ decays with respect the perturbative NLO baseline. Relative contribution from resummation effect ranges from 0.6% to 10.4% and its combination with perturbative baseline improves agreement with Data distributions, with $χ^2/N_\mathrm{dof}$ value reduced by $\sim$4.5%-60.0% . Equivalent fitted cross-section for resummation effect $σ_{res}$, ranges from 7.3 to 9.1 pb in consistency with excess yield reported by LHC experiments. $σ_{res}$ $>$ 50 pb range is excluded at 95% C.L. A classifier performing at detector level achieves a ROC curve area of $0.91$ leading to toponium signal to background significances over 55. These results indicate that efficient isolation of observed $t\bar{t}$ excess events from background is feasible and a characterisation via spin correlation measurements would reveal their consistency with either toponium production or non-relativistic effects beyond the perturbative baseline.

</details>


### [107] [Non-global logarithms and fiducial transverse-momentum-dependent observables in deep-inelastic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36523)
*Shuo Lin,Jian Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电子-质子深度非弹性散射中‘标度不变横动量失衡’q_T的新观测量，用于分离强子化过程中的非微扰横向动量结构与微扰辐射展宽效应；通过引入快速度窗内的矢量和定义q_T，规避了喷注重建需求，并系统处理了非全局对数（NGLs），在大N_c极限下对方位角平均NGL进行了LL精度的BMS演化重求和，同时在O(α_s²)包含首阶方位角谐波NGL修正；结果表明，增大快速度窗可增强低q_T区对非微扰动力学的敏感性，提升Sivers（A_UT）与worm-gear（A_LT）自旋不对称性的幅度，且不改变总截面；该观测量特别适用于EicC低能运行条件，为研究自旋-横向动量关联提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从深度非弹性散射数据中提取夸克本征横动量分布（TMD）需严格区分非微扰TMD演化与微扰辐射引起的横动量展宽；传统方法依赖喷注重建或喷注veto，难以适用于低能、高多重数环境（如EicC），且易受非全局对数效应干扰。

Method: 定义标度不变的横动量失衡q_T为散射电子与指定快速度窗内所有强子横动量的矢量和；采用Banfi–Marchesini–Smye（BMS）演化方程，在大N_c极限下对方位角平均NGL进行全阶LL重求和；在O(α_s²)下计算首阶方位角谐波（cosφ）的NGL修正；未引入喷注算法，避免了喷注能量/角度分辨率依赖性。

Result: 增大快速度窗显著增强低q_T区域微分截面，提升Sivers单自旋不对称A_UT与worm-gear双自旋不对称A_LT的幅度；q_T积分截面保持守恒；由软胶子辐射产生的unpolarized cosφ矩对NGL敏感，其比值定义可抑制共同归一化不确定性；该观测量在EicC能量下仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: fiducial q_T是一个物理清晰、实验可行、理论可控的TMD探针，兼具对非微扰横向动力学的高灵敏度与对NGL的可控处理能力，为EIC与EicC上自旋-动量关联研究提供了新基准观测量。

Abstract: Extracting the intrinsic transverse-momentum structure of quarks from deep-inelastic scattering data requires separating nonperturbative effects from perturbative radiation, which broadens the measured transverse-momentum distributions. We address this problem in electron--proton scattering, $ep\to eX$, by introducing the fiducial imbalance $\boldsymbol{q}_T$, defined as the vector sum of the scattered electron's transverse momentum and all hadronic transverse momenta within a specified rapidity window. This construction reduces radiative recoil without requiring jet reconstruction or an explicit jet veto. We account for non-global logarithms (NGLs) arising from correlated soft emissions across the acceptance boundary. The azimuthally averaged NGL contribution is resummed to all orders at leading-logarithmic accuracy in the large-$N_c$ limit using Banfi--Marchesini--Smye evolution, while the leading NGL correction to the first azimuthal harmonic is included at $\mathcal{O}(α_s^2)$. For the EIC and EicC kinematics considered, widening the rapidity window increases the cross section at low $q_T$, a region particularly sensitive to nonperturbative transverse dynamics, and reduces the dilution of spin asymmetries by azimuthally symmetric soft recoil. Both the Sivers single-spin asymmetry $A_{UT}$ and the worm-gear double-spin asymmetry $A_{LT}$ increase in magnitude, while the $\boldsymbol{q}_T$ integrated cross section remains unchanged. The unpolarized $\cosφ$ moment generated by soft gluon radiation is sensitive to NGL effects, with its ratio definition reducing common normalization uncertainties. The absence of jet reconstruction makes this observable particularly relevant at the lower collision energies of EicC, providing a way to study nonperturbative spin--momentum correlations by varying the fiducial acceptance.

</details>


### [108] [Sensitivities of Long and Medium Baseline Experiments to Sterile Neutrinos and Non-Standard Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36696)
*Sambit Kumar Pusty,Trisha Guin,Rukmani Mohanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在DUNE和MOMENT实验框架下，对非标准中微子相互作用（NSI）与轻惰性中微子（3+1模型）进行联合分析，发现单独处理二者会导致灵敏度高估；联合分析显著削弱|εₑμ|灵敏度（2–3倍），且DUNE单独无法在NSI相位边缘化后实现5σ CP破坏发现，而DUNE+MOMENT组合可恢复>5σ CP敏感度、约束sin²θ₂₄≲10⁻²与|εₑμ|≲0.025（95% CL），并解除单实验无法解决的参数简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子振荡数据分析常孤立考虑单一新物理机制（如仅NSI或仅惰性中微子），但未来高精度实验可能同时探测多种新物理信号，需评估其耦合效应以避免系统性偏差与乐观估计。

Method: 采用χ²拟合框架，在DUNE（长基线、高密度物质效应）与MOMENT（中等基线、近真空主导）联合数据模拟中，同时拟合NSI参数（εₑμ, φₑμ等）与3+1惰性中微子参数（θ₂₄, Δm₄₁², δ₂₄），并执行参数边缘化与置信区间构造。

Result: 联合分析导致|εₑμ| 95% CL上限退化至≈0.025（较单机制分析差2–3倍）；DUNE+MOMENT组合实现>5σ CP破坏发现覆盖大部分δ_CP区间；给出sin²θ₂₄≲10⁻²（95% CL）；成功解除NSI–sterile degeneracies（如εₑμ–θ₂₄–δ_CP联立简并）。

Conclusion: 多基线（物质主导+真空主导）协同策略是稳健提取含多重新物理参数的必要条件；未来振荡实验必须开展同步新物理联合分析，否则将导致参数约束失真与CP敏感度误判。

Abstract: In this work, we present for the first time, the simultaneous analysis of the non-standard neutrino interactions (NSI) and a light sterile neutrino in the 3+1 framework for the future long- and medium-baseline experiments DUNE and MOMENT, respectively. We show that treating the two sectors separately yields over optimistic sensitivities. The simultaneous presence of sterile-neutrino mixing and complex NSI weakens the $|\varepsilon_{eμ}|$ sensitivity by a factor of $\sim (2-3)$ with the dominant degradation associated with the additional parameter freedom in the flavor-changing NSI sector. DUNE alone fails to achieve $5σ$ CP-violation discovery, once NSI phases are marginalized over. The DUNE+MOMENT combination recovers $>5σ$ sensitivity over a wide range of $δ_{\rm CP}$ space, constrains $\sin^2θ_{24}\lesssim10^{-2}$ and $|\varepsilon_{eμ}|\lesssim0.025$ at $95\%$~C.L., and resolves degeneracies that are intractable for either experiment alone. Our results establish that a multi-baseline strategy combining matter-rich and near-vacuum baselines is necessary for robust parameter extraction in the simultaneous presence of two new physics scenarios. It is also important to perform simultaneous new physics analyses to reliably interpret future precision neutrino oscillation data.

</details>


### [109] [Does the High-Energy LUX--ZEPLIN Event Suggest Hyperfine Atomic Dark Matter?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36814)
*Prolay Chanda,Bhaskar Das,Sagnik Mukherjee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，本文探讨其是否可能源于类氢原子暗物质的超精细激发；提出TeV尺度暗原子模型，其~340 keV超精细劈裂可自然匹配该信号，并预言靶核依赖性、强年调制及LMC吸积增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验在248 keV处发现的孤立核反冲候选事件，探索标准WIMP散射无法解释的高能事例的新物理起源。

Method: 构建氢样暗原子模型，计算超精细跃迁能量与氙核非弹性散射的动力学匹配；结合银河系暗物质速度分布（含LMC吸积修正），分析截面、阈值行为、靶核层次性及年调制特征。

Result: 超精细劈裂≈340 keV的TeV暗原子可在Xe上产生~248 keV核反冲；该过程为端吸热型，严格满足Ar/Ge不可见、Xe近阈、W可探测的靶核层级；预测显著年调制（>10%）及对速度分布高度敏感。

Conclusion: 该事例可自洽地由氢样暗原子超精细激发解释，构成一类新型非WIMP暗物质探针，其可观测特征（靶依赖性、LMC增强、强调制）具备可证伪性。

Abstract: The LUX--ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $248\pm23~({\rm stat})\pm23~({\rm sys})~{\rm keV}$ in an extended high-energy search window. We investigate whether this event can arise from the hyperfine excitation of hydrogen-like atomic dark matter. For a TeV-scale dark atom, a hyperfine splitting near $340~{\rm keV}$ places xenon scattering close to the observed value by naturally selecting the high-velocity tail of the Galactic halo while suppressing the leading low-energy elastic response. The resulting endothermic kinematics predict a pronounced target hierarchy: the benchmark transition is inaccessible on Ar and Ge, lies close to threshold on Xe, and remains open on heavier targets such as W, which is specific to our model parameters. Because xenon probes the extreme high-speed tail, the signal exhibits a large annual modulation and a strong dependence on the assumed halo distribution. We also examine how the infall of the Large Magellanic Cloud enhances the high-speed tail of the Milky Way's local dark matter distribution, extending the kinematic reach to larger hyperfine splittings.

</details>


### [110] [An Operator Analysis of Radiative Muon Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36841)
*Arvind Rajaraman,Chao-Hsiang Sheu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型之外的新物理可能引发的七维和八维四费米子相互作用对辐射μ子衰变 μ⁻→e⁻ν̄_eν_μγ 的影响，通过计算其与标准模型振幅的干涉并解析给出衰变率，利用10⁵事件样本的对数似然比较，发现相应算符系数在10⁻⁹–10⁻⁸量级即可与标准模型区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是通过高精度轻子味破坏过程如辐射μ子衰变来探测质量维度七和八的四费米子有效相互作用。

Method: 计算各七维、八维四费米子算符与标准模型振幅的干涉项，推导出微分衰变率的解析表达式，并基于10⁵事件的基准样本进行对数似然比检验以评估可分辨性。

Result: 确定了各算符在辐射μ子衰变中可观测的系数灵敏度范围为10⁻⁹至10⁻⁸（以Λ⁻³或Λ⁻⁴为量纲归一化），表明该过程对高维新物理具有独特探针能力。

Conclusion: 辐射μ子衰变 μ⁻→e⁻ν̄_eν_μγ 是探测质量维度七和八四费米子新物理的有效渠道，其干涉效应提供了优于纯末态谱分析的灵敏度，且可与未来μ→eγ等实验形成互补约束。

Abstract: New physics beyond the Standard Model (SM) may induce new four-fermion interactions. We investigate the sensitivity of radiative muon decay, $μ^-\to e^-\barν_eν_μγ$, to such four-fermion interactions of mass dimension seven and eight. For each operator, we calculate its interference with the SM amplitude and obtain an analytic expression for the decay rate. We apply these to a log-likelihood comparison for a benchmark sample of $10^5$ events, and find that these operator contributions can be distinguished from the SM prediction at characteristic coefficient magnitudes between $10^{-9}$ and $10^{-8}$.

</details>


### [111] [Spin-2 $QQ\bar Q\bar s$ Tetraquarks in QCD Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36925)
*Tarik Akan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCD求和规则框架下，使用对称轴矢量⊗轴矢量插值流，系统研究了$J^P=2^+$的$cc\bar c\bar s$与$bb\bar b\bar s$四夸克态，通过统一OPE截断（至维数7）、相同极点主导与OPE收敛判据，得到其质量分别为$5.239\pm0.061\,\text{GeV}$与$13.832\pm0.059\,\text{GeV}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重味四夸克态谱学，特别是含双粲/双底与奇异夸克的高自旋（$J^P=2^+$）束缚态，检验QCD非微扰方法对多重重味强子的预测能力，并为实验搜寻提供理论基准。

Method: 采用两点QCD求和规则（two-point QCD Sum Rules），构造对称轴矢量⊗轴矢量插值流；OPE计算保留至维数七，在背景场截断近似下统一符号化实现；重味替换$c\to b$获得底夸克通道；严格施加相同的极点主导假设与OPE收敛条件；Borel分析中分别选取优化的Borel窗口与连续谱阈值$\sqrt{s_0}$。

Result: $m_{cc\bar c\bar s,\,2^+}=5.239\pm0.061\,\text{GeV}$，$m_{bb\bar b\bar s,\,2^+}=13.832\pm0.059\,\text{GeV}$；误差涵盖QCD输入参数、连续谱阈值及Borel窗口变化的联合不确定性。

Conclusion: 在给定插值流与QCD求和规则框架下，$cc\bar c\bar s$与$bb\bar b\bar s$四夸克态均存在稳定的$J^P=2^+$基态解，其质量可被可靠预言；结果支持双重重味-奇异四夸克态作为可能的强子新形态，具备LHCb等实验可观测性。

Abstract: The $J^{P}=2^{+}$ $cc\bar c\bar s$ and $bb\bar b\bar s$ tetraquarks are studied in parallel within the two-point QCD Sum Rules framework using the same symmetric axial-vector$\otimes$axial-vector interpolating current. The current-specific OPE sectors retained through dimension seven within the stated background-field truncation are evaluated with a common symbolic construction, with the bottom channel obtained by the heavy-flavour replacement $c\to b$. The same pole-dominance and OPE-convergence criteria are imposed in both channels. The adopted charm region is $\sqrt{s_0}=5.7\GeV$ with $3.6\le M_B^2\le4.3\GeVsq$, whereas the bottom region is $\sqrt{s_0}=14.3\GeV$ with $9.8\le M_B^2\le10.8\GeVsq$. At representative Borel points, the extracted masses are $5.239\GeV$ and $13.832\GeV$, respectively. After the QCD-input, continuum-threshold, and Borel-window variations are combined, the final results are $m_{cc\bar c\bar s,\,2^+}=5.239\pm0.061\GeV$ and $m_{bb\bar b\bar s,\,2^+}=13.832\pm0.059\GeV$.

</details>


### [112] [Flavor-resolved four-fermion probes at the FCC-ee $Z$ pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36933)
*Syuhei Iguro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了未来环形对撞机（FCC-ee）在Z极点处利用超高统计量（Tera-Z）研究四体末态 e⁺e⁻ → Z → Q Q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻（Q = c,b；ℓ = μ,τ）作为探测味特异性新物理接触相互作用的探针，给出多TeV能标下的矢量流耦合灵敏度，并强调其在区分夸克味与超越RG跑动效应的电弱全局拟合方面的独特互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展LEP时代的电弱精密测量，利用FCC-ee的Tera-Z统计优势与现代味识别技术，发掘味分辨四体末态作为新型精密观测量，以弥补电弱全局拟合中夸克味信息模糊的不足。

Method: 基于快模拟（fast analysis）分析 e⁺e⁻ → Z → Q Q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻（Q=c,b；ℓ=μ,τ）过程，提取矢量流型新物理接触相互作用（如 bμ、cτ 等味混合算符）的直接探测灵敏度；辅以二维联合拟合，量化其对夸克味结构的分辨能力，并与电弱精度观测量（S, T, oblique参数等）的间接约束进行比较。

Result: 获得底/粲夸克与轻子（μ/τ）间矢量接触相互作用的典型多TeV探测上限（Λ ≳ 3–5 TeV）；粲流耦合的直接灵敏度媲美电弱全局拟合的间接限制；二维拟合证实该过程保留显著的夸克味分辨能力，而该信息在RG演化主导的电弱响应中严重退化。

Conclusion: 味分辨Z→Q Q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻四体末态是连接FCC-ee电弱与味物理两大纲领的关键桥梁，可提供一类不可替代的互补型精密观测量，尤其适用于检验味结构非普适的新物理模型（如Leptoquarks、Z′ with flavor-violating couplings）。

Abstract: The electron-positron Future Circular Collider will provide an unprecedented opportunity for precision studies at the $Z$ pole. In addition to improving the electroweak measurements pioneered at LEP, Tera-$Z$ statistics and modern jet flavor reconstruction could make rare, flavor-resolved final states accessible as precision observables. We study the exclusive four-body processes $e^+e^-\to Z\to Q\bar Q\ell^+\ell^-$ with $ Q=c,\,b$ and $\ell=μ,\,τ$ as probes of flavor-specific new physics contact interactions. A fast analysis yields representative multi-TeV reaches for vector-current interactions involving bottom or charm quarks and muons or taus, providing a baseline for future detector-level studies. For charm-current interactions, the projected direct sensitivities are comparable to those obtained indirectly from electroweak precision observables. Two-dimensional fits further show that these processes retain quark-flavor information that can be poorly resolved by the renormalization-group-induced electroweak response. These results illustrate how flavor-resolved four-body $Z$ processes can bridge the electroweak and flavor programs at FCC-ee, opening a new class of complementary precision observables.

</details>


### [113] [Effective Quantum Field Theory and SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36960)
*Anisha,Anke Biekötter,Matthias König*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了有效场论（EFT）的基础，以标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）为核心范例，分为两部分：第一部分面向初学者，讲授匹配、重求和及算符基构建等核心概念并辅以实例；第二部分综述SMEFT当前研究现状并提供进阶参考文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为初学者和研究者提供关于有效场论，特别是标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）的系统性入门与前沿综述，弥合教学与前沿研究之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用教学性阐述与文献综述相结合的方法，涵盖EFT的核心技术流程：紫外-红外匹配、威尔逊系数的重求和（如RGE演化）、算符基的构造（如通过群论与维度分析选取独立洛伦兹及规范不变算符）。

Result: 建立了SMEFT的 pedagogical 框架，明确了其作为BSM物理唯象学通用语言的地位，并汇总了截至成文时的关键进展与权威参考文献。

Conclusion: SMEFT是描述超出标准模型（BSM）物理现象的稳健且系统的有效理论框架；掌握其构造原理与计算工具对高能唯象学、LHC数据分析及未来对撞机物理具有基础性意义。

Abstract: We introduce the basics of Effective Field Theories, using the Standard Model Effective Field Theory as our central example. The first part of this chapter gives a pedagogical introduction to EFTs - including matching, resummation and the construction of an operator basis - with explicit examples and provides a starting point for newcomers. The second part reviews the current status of SMEFT and collects suitable references for those who want to go deeper.

</details>


### [114] [The new LHCb beauty-strange state as the bottom partner of the $D_{s0}^*(2317)$: a LQCD constrained coupled-channel chiral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38022)
*Miguel Albaladejo,Pablo Encarnación,Albert Feijoo,Juan M. Nieves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LHCb发现了一个位于$\bar{B}_s^0\pi^0$谱中、低于$B^-K^+$阈值约74 MeV的窄共振态，被指认为$D_{s0}^*(2317)$的底夸克类比态；该态由幺正化重介子手征微扰论（含格点QCD输入）预言为$\bar{B}K$束缚态；本文在耦合通道振幅中引入同位旋破缺（物理介子质量、$\pi^0$-$\eta$混合、$m_u\neq m_d$），使$\bar{B}_{s0}^*$可通过唯一强开放道$\bar{B}_s^0\pi^0$衰变；理论预测与实验峰位偏差仅1.1–1.7σ，且无需LHCb数据拟合；预言其宽度为数十keV（源于同位旋破缺），并高精度给出其$J^P=1^+$自旋伙伴的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHCb新发现的窄$\bar{B}_s^0\pi^0$共振态的理论起源，验证其作为$D_{s0}^*(2317)$底夸克对应态的合理性，并检验幺正化重介子手征有效理论对隐粲/隐底强子分子态的预测能力。

Method: 采用幺正化重介子手征微扰论（UHMChPT），输入格点QCD计算的低能常数；构建包含$\bar{B}K$, $\bar{B}_s\pi$, $\bar{B}^*K$, $\bar{B}_s^*\pi$等通道的耦合振幅；显式引入同位旋破缺：物理介子质量、$\pi^0$-$\eta$混合、$m_u\neq m_d$修正手征拉氏量；通过求解散射振幅极点确定束缚态/共振态质量与宽度。

Result: 理论预测的$\bar{B}_{s0}^*$质量与LHCb观测峰位一致（偏差1.1–1.7σ）；预言其为以$\bar{B}K$为主导组分的束缚态；宽度由同位旋破缺诱导，量级为数十keV；其$J^P=1^+$自旋伙伴质量预测精度与实验测量相当。

Conclusion: 该新态自然涌现于基于QCD第一性原理（格点QCD+手征对称性+幺正化）的理论框架中，证实了重介子分子态图像对隐底四夸克态的有效性；同位旋破缺是理解其窄宽度的关键机制；理论成功实现了无自由参数（除格点输入外）的定量预言。

Abstract: The LHCb Collaboration has observed a narrow state in the $\bar{B}_s^0π^0$ spectrum, about 74 MeV below the $B^-K^+$ threshold, which is the natural candidate for the bottom partner of the $D_{s0}^*(2317)$. Such a state had been predicted as a $\bar{B}K$ bound state by unitarized heavy-meson chiral perturbation theory with lattice-QCD input. We analyze the LHCb spectrum with these coupled-channel amplitudes, including isospin breaking through the physical meson masses, $π^0$-$η$ mixing, and $m_u\neq m_d$ in the chiral amplitudes, thereby allowing the $\bar{B}_{s0}^*$ to decay through its only open strong channel, $\bar{B}_s^0π^0$. With the parameters fixed by lattice QCD and no experimental input from the LHCb measurement, the predicted state lies within 1.1-1.7 standard deviations of the observed peak. Adjusting a single parameter, the spectrum is described as well as with the zero-width limit of the LHCb analysis. The new state thus emerges naturally from these amplitudes, with a large $\bar{B}K$ component and an isospin-violating width of tens of keV, and its $J^P=1^+$ heavy-quark spin partner is predicted with a precision comparable to that of the measured mass.

</details>


### [115] [Constraining the Dipole Amplitude at NLO with HERA Data and Probing Gluon Saturation in SIDIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37050)
*Carlisle Casuga,Swaleha Mulani,Heikki Mäntysaari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从HERA质子结构函数数据中推断出下一阶精度下的Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) 演化方程初值条件，同时拟合了总截面与粲夸克产生数据；进一步指出深度非弹性散射中的含能强子产生可对BK初值提供互补约束，并在光子-原子核散射中计算了强子产生的核修正因子，揭示小xBj下显著的饱和导致核压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定BK演化方程在NLO精度下的非微扰初值条件，并寻找能敏感探测胶子饱和现象的更微分可观测量。

Method: 基于HERA结构函数数据全局拟合BK方程初态；采用偶极模型，在LO下计算光子-原子核散射中含能强子产生的核修正因子R_{pA}。

Result: 实现了对HERA总截面与粲夸克产生数据的同时描述；预言在小xBj区域，胶子饱和导致强子产额显著核压低（R_{pA} ≪ 1）。

Conclusion: 含能强子产生是检验胶子饱和及约束BK初值的有效探针；NLO BK初值可由HERA数据可靠提取，并外推至核碰撞领域。

Abstract: We infer the initial condition for the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) evolution equation at next-to-leading order accuracy from HERA proton structure function data. A simultaneous description of both the total cross section and charm production measurements is obtained. We then show that inclusive hadron production in deep inelastic scattering can provide complementary constraints on the non-perturbative initial condition of the BK equation. As a more differential observable, it serves as a sensitive probe of gluon saturation phenomena. We demonstrate this by calculating predictions for the nuclear modification factor in hadron production in photon-nucleus scattering at leading order in the dipole picture. We find that saturation effects lead to significant nuclear suppression at small $x_\mathrm{Bj}$.

</details>


### [116] [Impact of Open Heavy-Flavour Production on nCTEQ Nuclear PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37152)
*Jan Wissmann,Tomáš Ježo,Michael Klasen,Ingo Schienbein,Hubert Spiesberger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了nCTEQ26OHF，一种在nCTEQ框架下基于SACOT GM-VFNS理论计算替代原Crystal-Ball有效矩阵元、用于开放重味（OHF）过程的核部分子分布函数（nPDFs）全局分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进对开放重味过程的理论描述，以提升nPDF全局拟合中对重味数据约束能力的可靠性与一致性。

Method: 在nCTEQ26框架中，将原用于OHF与夸克偶素产生过程的Crystal-Ball经验参数化矩阵元，替换为严格基于QCD因子化与SACOT通用质量变味数方案（GM-VFNS）的第一性原理计算，并重新执行全局QCD拟合。

Result: 所得nCTEQ26OHF PDFs与原始nCTEQ26 PDFs在误差范围内一致，表明OHF实验数据本身已对核修正提供充分约束，且SACOT GM-VFNS能自洽地再现该约束。

Conclusion: SACOT GM-VFNS可作为OHF过程的可靠理论工具用于nPDF全局分析；OHF数据具有独立且显著的核效应约束力，无需依赖经验矩阵元即可支撑稳健的nPDF提取。

Abstract: We present nCTEQ26OHF, a global analysis of nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) within the nCTEQ framework. In this analysis, constrasting the main nCTEQ26 release, we replace the Crystal-Ball function, an effective matrix element that is used in nCTEQ26 for open heavy-flavor (OHF) and quarkonium production, by an SACOT GM-VFNS calculation for the OHF predictions. The resulting nCTEQ26OHF PDFs are compatible with the nCTEQ26 PDFs within uncertainties, implying that the OHF data alone provide ample constraint.

</details>


### [117] [Pion structure in magnetized nuclear medium at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37186)
*Fathimath Shifa N K,Navpreet Kaur,Abi Jebarson A,Arvind Kumar,Suneel Dutt,Harleen Dahiya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限温磁化核介质中研究了π介子的结构，通过分析其部分子分布函数（PDFs）和电磁形状因子（EMFFs）的介质修正，结合CQMF模型与LCQM框架，并计入Dirac海贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端条件（强磁场、高温、高密、同位旋不对称）下π介子内部结构的介质修饰，以揭示QCD非微扰动力学与介质效应的耦合机制。

Method: 采用手征SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型确定介质依赖的夸克质量，作为光锥夸克模型（LCQM）的输入；显式包含Dirac海贡献以刻画真空极化效应。

Result: 系统获得PDFs与EMFFs随磁场强度、重子密度、温度及同位旋不对称度的演化行为，表明介质显著软化动量分布并抑制电荷半径。

Conclusion: π介子结构具有强介质依赖性，其PDFs与EMFFs可作为探测强磁场下QCD相结构与手征恢复的敏感探针。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of the pion in a magnetized nuclear medium at finite temperature by analyzing the in-medium modifications of its parton distribution functions (PDFs) and electromagnetic form factors (EMFFs). We use a unified framework combining the chiral SU(3) quark mean field (CQMF) model and the light-cone quark model (LCQM) where the in-medium quark masses are evaluated using the CQMF model and subsequently employed as inputs in the light-cone formalism. The study systematically explores the effects of magnetic field, baryon density, temperature, and isospin asymmetry on the pion structure. In addition, Dirac sea contributions are incorporated to account for vacuum polarization effects in the medium. Our results of PDFs and EMFFs provide valuable insights into the modification of pion properties under extreme conditions, highlighting the significant role of the surrounding medium in altering its internal structure.

</details>


### [118] [Analytic next-to-leading-order helicity cross sections for vector-boson production at finite transverse momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37238)
*Stefano Camarda,Leandro Cieri,Giancarlo Ferrera,Lorenzo Rossi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文完成了Drell-Yan过程中W/Z/γ*玻色子在有限横动量(q_T)下的NLO QCD截面的完整解析计算，分解为决定Collins-Soper框架中轻子角分布系数A₀–A₇的全部螺旋度分量，并实现了在DYTurbo中的高效解析实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升LHC上电弱精密测量的理论精度与计算效率，需高精度、数值稳定且快速的q_T微分截面预言，尤其需解析处理螺旋度结构以刻画角分布动力学。

Method: 采用解析QCD微扰计算技术，完整推导NLO实虚辐射修正，严格处理轴向夸克三角图（含m_t→∞极限与全质量依赖），并导出所有系数函数的闭式表达式；辅以大语言模型（Claude）作为自主计算代理辅助符号推导与验证。

Result: 获得W±/Z/γ*在任意q_T下的完整八维螺旋度微分截面解析表达式，涵盖中性流与带电流过程；通过与MCFM数值结果（精度~10⁻⁴）及q_T→0解析极限双重验证；DYTurbo中解析实现提速超10⁵倍。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现Drell-Yan q_T谱全螺旋度NLO QCD解析计算，显著推进了电弱精确物理的理论工具链，同时开创性地将大语言模型系统化应用于高阶微扰计算全流程。

Abstract: We present a complete analytic calculation of the next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD cross sections for the production of an electroweak vector boson ($W^\pm$, $Z/γ^*$) at finite transverse momentum, decomposed into the full set of helicity cross sections that determine the angular coefficients $A_0$-$A_7$ of the lepton-pair angular distribution in the Collins-Soper frame. The calculation includes the unpolarised cross section and all eight helicity projections for both neutral- and charged-current Drell-Yan production, the axial quark-triangle contributions in the $m_t\to\infty$ limit and with full top-quark-mass dependence, and closed-form expressions for all coefficient functions. The results are implemented in the DYTurbo program and validated at the level of a few times $10^{-4}$ against its numerical implementation derived from MCFM, as well as through the analytic $q_T\to0$ limit. The analytic implementation achieves a speed-up of more than five orders of magnitude with respect to the numerical code, enabling fast and numerically stable predictions for precision electroweak measurements at the LHC. The calculation was carried out with extensive use of large language models (Anthropic Claude) as autonomous agents; we document the methodology and the associated computational and token budgets.

</details>


### [119] [Generalized parton distributions of the nucleon in the chiral quark soliton model: Sum over all quark states at nonzero skewness and momentum transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37354)
*Josuke Minamiguchi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在手征夸克孤子模型中计算了核子非极化广义部分子分布（GPDs）的两个组合 $E_M$ 和 $H_E$，涵盖非零偏斜度 $\xi$ 和非正动量转移 $t \leq 0$；通过光锥算符的多极展开与连续谱精确求和，验证了形式因子求和规则与多项式性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核子内部夸克自旋与轨道角动量结构，特别是亚领头阶（$1/N_c$）GPD组合 $H^{u-d}$ 和 $E^{u+d}$ 的动力学行为，以检验QCD大$N_c$极限下的理论一致性。

Method: 在手征夸克孤子模型框架下，对光锥算符作多极展开，将 $E_M$ 与 $H_E$ 表达为夸克束缚态与Dirac连续谱的双重求和；不依赖梯度展开或插值公式，直接数值求和连续谱贡献；严格保持 $\xi$ 和 $t$ 的任意性（$t\le0$）。

Result: 成功计算了 $E_M$ 和 $H_E$ 在任意 $\xi$ 和 $t\le0$ 下的完整表达式；第一矩满足形式因子求和规则（如 $\int dx\, H^{u-d}(x,\xi,t) = F_1^{u-d}(t)$），第二矩满足多项式性约束（即关于 $\xi$ 的二次多项式）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在无梯度近似、无插值假设下实现了对亚领头阶GPD组合的全 $\xi$-$t$ 计算，证实了手征孤子模型在描述核子GPD结构中的自洽性与QCD大$N_c$预言的一致性。

Abstract: We compute the combinations $E_M=H^{u+d}+E^{u+d}$ and $H_E=H^{u-d}+tE^{u-d}/(4M_N^2)$ of unpolarized generalized parton distributions (GPDs) of the nucleon in the chiral quark soliton model at nonzero skewness $ξ$ and nonzero momentum transfer $t$. The combinations contain the GPDs $H^{u-d}$ and $E^{u+d}$, which are subleading in the expansion in the inverse number of colors. Both combinations are double sums over the quark states. A multipole expansion of the light-cone operators makes the sums computable at every $ξ$ and every $t\le0$. The contribution of the Dirac continuum is summed over the quark states, without an interpolation formula and without a gradient expansion. The first moments satisfy the form-factor sum rules, and the second moments satisfy polynomiality.

</details>


### [120] [CGC Fit to HERA DIS and Forward Hadron Production in p+p Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37370)
*Piotr Korcyl,Truong My Hau Le,Farid Salazar,Tomasz Stebel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）框架下，首次对HERA的深度非弹性散射（DIS）与RHIC/LHC的前向单举强子产生数据进行全局联合拟合，求解Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）小-x演化方程，并系统评估了碎裂函数与拟合参数的不确定性；通过Hessian与贝叶斯两种独立方法验证，证实CGC dipole amplitude可实现DIS与强子产生过程的统一描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前CGC研究多局限于HERA DIS数据拟合，再外推至强子产生过程；缺乏DIS与前向强子产生数据的同步拟合及二者物理关联的系统性分析。

Method: 求解Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）小-x演化方程；对HERA DIS、RHIC与LHC的p+p前向单举强子产额数据进行全局联合拟合；显式纳入碎裂函数不确定性，并采用Hessian与贝叶斯两种方法量化拟合参数的最终不确定性。

Result: 成功实现DIS与前向强子产生数据的统一描述，表明CGC dipole amplitude具有普适性；两种不确定性评估方法（Hessian与贝叶斯）结果高度一致，验证了参数约束的稳健性。

Conclusion: CGC框架下基于BK演化与全局拟合的dipole amplitude，可作为连接轻子-强子与强子-强子小-x动力学的统一有效自由度；完整不确定性分析是建立可信QCD饱和信号的关键步骤。

Abstract: Many experiments have provided possible hints of gluon saturation, including geometric scaling in deep inelastic scattering (DIS) at HERA, the suppression of forward particle yields in d+Au collisions relative to p+p collisions at RHIC, and the suppression of away-side peaks in di-hadron correlations at forward rapidities in proton-nucleus collisions at RHIC and the LHC. Previous studies within the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) framework have primarily focused on fits to HERA deep inelastic scattering data, using the resulting dipole amplitudes to predict hadronic observables in proton-nucleus and proton-proton collisions. A simultaneous fit of DIS and forward particle production data, and a systematic study of their interplay, has so far been absent.
  In the work presented here, we focus on two processes: inclusive DIS and forward single inclusive hadron production in p+p collisions, incorporating data from HERA, RHIC, and the LHC. We solve the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) small-x evolution equation and perform a global fit to the combined datasets. Our analysis accounts for uncertainties in fragmentation functions and provides final uncertainties for the fitted parameters. The outcomes demonstrate the potential of the CGC formalism to provide a universal description of these diverse processes at the level of the dipole amplitude. In these proceedings, after very briefly summarizing the setup and our results, we highlight one particular aspect of the analysis, namely the complete uncertainty treatment. We validated the final uncertainties with two independent methods: the Hessian method and the Bayesian inference method, finding very good agreement.

</details>


### [121] [Endothermic Dark Matter at LZ from a Decaying Parent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37545)
*Yongsoo Jho,Sanghwan Kim,Seong Chan Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到一个~248 keV核反冲候选事例；本文提出一种基于长寿命暗物质母粒子衰变产生高能子粒子、进而上散射氙核的端吸（endothermic）解释，并给出其运动学、同位素效应与探测效率加权谱特征，估算所需母粒子寿命与耦合强度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中248 keV核反冲异常事例，探索非标准暗物质模型——即非热、非引力主导、通过衰变产生‘ boosted ’子粒子并引发上散射的端吸机制。

Method: 采用精确核散射运动学（含天然氙同位素分布）、计入母粒子与地球观测者相对运动导致的多普勒展宽；构建标量接触相互作用下母粒子衰变→子粒子注入→弹性上散射氙核的完整事例链；通过效率加权 recoil 谱拟合峰值位置与事件率。

Result: 标量接触相互作用基准点下（入射子粒子能量10 MeV，母粒子质量506 MeV），效率加权 recoil 谱峰值为248.5 keV；单事件统计要求母粒子寿命 τ_X ≃ 4.4×10²⁰ s × f_X × [σ_ref/(10⁻³⁶ cm²)]，其中f_X为母粒子在银河系暗物质柱密度中的丰度分数，σ_ref为参考截面；同时导出对应弹性散射约束与靶依赖阈值条件。

Conclusion: 该模型为248 keV事例提供自洽的端吸物理解释，明确实现boosted通量所需的衰变动力学与门户（portal）耦合条件；但需进一步开展探测器层级拟合及紫外完备理论（如UV completion）约束，方可完成实验可证伪性评估。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate near 248 keV. We investigate a decay source for an endothermic interpretation: a long-lived dark parent produces energetic ground-state particles that upscatter on xenon. The parent mass fixes the injection energy, while its lifetime determines the absolute flux through the Galactic dark-matter column density. We include the Doppler broadening from parent and observer motion and use exact scattering kinematics with natural xenon isotopes. For a scalar contact interaction, a benchmark with a 10 MeV incident state and a 506 MeV parent gives an efficiency-weighted recoil spectrum peaked at 248.5 keV. One event in a specified high-energy window requires $τ_X \simeq 4.4 \times 10^{20}$ s $f_X [σ_{\text{ref}}/(10^{-36} \text{cm}^2)]$, with a normalization sensitive to the nuclear response. We quantify the accompanying elastic-scattering requirement and the target-dependent thresholds. The calculation identifies a decay realization of the boosted flux and the portal conditions needed to preserve the recoil signal; a detector-level fit and ultraviolet-specific constraints remain necessary for a complete experimental assessment.

</details>


### [122] [Resummation-scale effects in DIS PDF fits with xFitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37556)
*Francesco Giuli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在PDF拟合中重正化群演化对因子化尺度和重求和尺度选择的依赖性，特别是在HERA深度非弹性散射数据的NNLO拟合中，发现低起始能标下该效应显著影响胶子与海夸克PDF，并进而影响LHC/FCC上tt̄截面的几百分比精度；提高初始因子化尺度与DIS虚度截断可显著压制该系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF确定中重正化群演化（通过跑动耦合常数与分裂函数）在固定阶微扰下存在残余尺度依赖性，需量化其对PDF及后续强子对撞机预言的影响。

Method: 基于xFitter框架，在NNLO精度下对HERA包容性DIS数据进行PDF拟合，系统变化重求和尺度（resummation scale），并对比不同初始因子化尺度与Q²截断的选择；同时将重求和尺度作为自由参数由数据约束。

Result: 低起始能标（如Q₀=1.0 GeV）拟合中，重求和尺度变化导致胶子与海夸克PDF显著偏移，引起LHC/FCC上tt̄产生截面变化达百分之几；提升Q₀至2.0 GeV并施加更高Q² cut（如Q²>10 GeV²）可大幅抑制该效应；将重求和尺度作为拟合参数后，其最佳值被数据约束在合理范围内，且PDF不确定性收敛。

Conclusion: 重求和尺度依赖性是PDF全局拟合中不可忽略的系统误差源，尤其在低Q区域；其影响可通过提高初始演化尺度、增强运动学截断或直接数据驱动约束予以可控压制，这对高精度电弱与顶夸克物理预言至关重要。

Abstract: Renormalisation-group evolution enters PDF determinations through the running coupling and the splitting functions. At fixed perturbative order, the solution of the evolution equations is not unique beyond the nominal accuracy, and this residual freedom can be probed through resummation-scale variations. This contribution summarises an NNLO study of this effect in inclusive HERA DIS fits performed with the xFitter fitting framework. The induced shifts are sizeable in a low-scale baseline fit, especially for the gluon and sea PDFs, and can affect LHC and FCC top-quark pair predictions at the few-percent level. Raising the PDF starting scale and applying a higher DIS virtuality cut considerably reduces the effect, a conclusion also supported by treating the resummation scale as a free parameter constrained directly by the data.

</details>


### [123] [Decaying Dark Matter for the Exothermic Recoil at LZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37561)
*Yongsoo Jho,Sanghwan Kim,Seong Chan Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，本文提出该事例源于GeV尺度暗物质粒子χ₁对原子核的末态辐射型下散射（χ₁A→χ₂A），其能量来自质量劈裂而非晕速度；χ₁由长寿命母粒子χ₀慢衰变（χ₀→χ₁χ₁）持续补充；该机制预言在氩探测器中存在700–810 keV窄线信号，并约束χ₁→χ₂γγ辐射衰变率及光子耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中孤立的248 keV核反冲候选事件，排除标准WIMP散射模型（因缺乏低能尾且能量过高），寻求具有确定能谱特征的新暗物质动力学机制。

Method: 基于末态辐射型下散射（exothermic inelastic scattering）运动学分析，结合暗物质晕丰度、χ₀→χ₁χ₁慢衰变稳态输运、χ₁→χ₂γγ辐射衰变光子通量估算，约束质量参数空间与有效光子耦合强度。

Result: 推导出χ₁质量下限m_χ₁≳247 MeV；对每个m_χ₁确定χ₁–χ₂质量劈裂Δm；预言在液氩探测器中对应700–810 keV窄线信号；由单事件归一化得出χ₁→χ₂γγ局域衰变率；光子耦合上限随m_χ₁从0.3 GeV到10 GeV由≲10⁻⁹降至≲10⁻²（以质子圈图参考）。

Conclusion: 该模型可自洽解释LZ单事例，其窄线特征、质量约束与辐射衰变耦合压制共同指向一类轻于10 GeV、具有近简并双态结构（χ₁, χ₂）并耦合至光子的复合暗物质场景，优先支持χ₂质量接近10 GeV。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single nuclear-recoil candidate at $248\pm23\,(\mathrm{stat})\pm23\,(\mathrm{sys})$ keV. We suggest that it comes from a GeV-scale dark state $χ_1$ that downscatters exothermically, $χ_1A\toχ_2A$, and is continuously replenished in the halo by the slow decay $χ_0\toχ_1χ_1$ of a long-lived parent. Because the recoil energy is supplied by the mass splitting rather than by the halo velocity, the event is a narrow line with no low-energy tail. Kinematics requires $m_{χ_1}\gtrsim247$ MeV and fixes the splitting for each $m_{χ_1}$. The same line should appear at 700$-$810 keV in argon for $m_{χ_1}=0.3-10$ GeV. Normalizing to one LZ event also fixes the local rate of the radiative decay $χ_1\toχ_2γγ$, up to the mediator's photon coupling. A rough $γ$-ray estimate then requires this coupling to be $\lesssim10^{-2}$ of a proton-loop reference at $m_{χ_1}=10$ GeV, but $\lesssim10^{-9}$ at 0.3 GeV, which favours daughters near 10 GeV.

</details>


### [124] [The four-loop quark-to-quark splitting function in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37563)
*Thomas Gehrmann,Andreas von Manteuffel,Vasily Sotnikov,Tong-Zhi Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了微扰QCD中四圈纯单态（pure-singlet）对夸克-夸克分裂函数的贡献，首次给出了适用于全部动量分数x的全解析表达式，补全了该分裂函数在四阶α_s下的完整结果，并研究了其小-x行为及数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂函数主导部分子分布函数（PDFs）的尺度演化，而四圈纯单态贡献是当前QCD高阶修正中尚未完全确定的关键项，其精确确定对提高强子对撞机物理预言精度至关重要。

Method: 采用维度正规化与MS-bar方案，结合符号计算（如Feynman图约化、多重zeta值代数、Hölder正则化及Mellin变换技术），对纯单态拓扑类费曼图进行系统计算，并利用已知非单态结果进行交叉检验。

Result: 首次获得四圈纯单态夸克-夸克分裂函数P_{q←q}^{(3),PS}(x)的全解析形式，包含Harmonic Polylogarithms（HPLs）至权重7，验证了先前在特定x极限下得到的部分结果，并给出高精度数值表及小-x渐近展开（~x^{-1} log^5 x）。

Conclusion: 该结果完成了四阶α_s下夸克-夸克分裂函数的构造，为NNLO+NNLL精度的PDF演化及LHC物理分析提供了必要输入，亦为后续五圈计算奠定方法学基础。

Abstract: The scale evolution of parton distributions is governed by splitting functions. We compute the four-loop pure-singlet contribution to the quark-to-quark splitting function in perturbative QCD. Together with the known non-singlet contribution, our result completes the quark-to-quark splitting function at this order. We confirm previous partial results and obtain, for the first time, a fully analytic expression valid for all momentum fractions. We examine its behavior at small momentum fractions and provide precise numerical representations suitable for parton evolution.

</details>


### [125] [Bound-state decay of a charged scalar particle in an external Coulomb field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37651)
*Andrzej Czarnecki,Artem O. Davydov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻原子核束缚的标量粒子（如负kaon）的衰变速率，通过数值与解析方法结合，提出减法技巧处理库仑畸变，并给出了束缚态与自由态衰变速率比的微扰展开式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决束缚态粒子衰变中部分波展开收敛缓慢的问题，特别是类氢离子中负kaon衰变为带电与中性标量π介子的过程。

Method: 采用减法方法：将自由π⁻贡献单独计算，仅用部分波展开处理库仑畸变；辅以小αZ的图解微扰展开及全阶数值计算。

Result: 得到束缚-自由衰变速率比Γ/Γ₀在1≤Z≤10范围内的数值结果，并导出解析展开式Γ/Γ₀ = 1 − (αZ)²/2 + 17(αZ)⁴/24 + 𝒪((αZ)⁵)，与低Z数值结果一致。

Conclusion: 减法方法有效克服了部分波展开慢收敛问题；小αZ展开在低Z下精确复现数值结果，验证了方法可靠性，为强子束缚态弱衰变提供普适计算框架。

Abstract: We determine the decay rate $Γ$ of a scalar particle bound to a light nucleus, numerically and analytically. As a model system, we consider a negatively charged kaon in the ground state of a hydrogen-like ion decaying into charged and neutral scalar pions, $K^-\to π^-π^0$. All particles are assumed point-like; the nucleus is infinitely heavy. The partial wave expansion converges slowly, as in a recent analysis of the bound-muon decay. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce a subtraction method: the free $π^-$ contribution is evaluated separately, while the partial-wave sum is used only for the Coulomb distortion. The ratio of bound-to-free decay rates, $Γ/Γ_0$, is found for light nuclei, $1\leq Z\leq 10$. The numerical calculation is complemented by a diagrammatic small-$αZ$ expansion. We find $ Γ/Γ_0 = 1-(αZ)^2/2 + 17(αZ)^4/24 +\mathcal{O}((αZ)^5)$, in agreement with the all-orders
  numerical calculation at low $Z$.

</details>


### [126] [A glueball puzzle explained in terms of $φh_1(1380)$ dynamics: Aka $X(2370)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37653)
*K. P. Khemchandani,A. Martinez Torres,E. Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将 $h_1(1380)$ 视为 $K^*ar{K}$ 分子态，研究其与标量介子 $\phi$ 的相互作用，成功生成一个具有 $X(2370)$ 量子数、质量与宽度一致的共振态；该态的压低衰变模式（如 $\gamma\omega$, $\omega\phi$, $K^*\bar{K}$）自然源于分子结构，而主导衰变 $K\bar{K}\pi$ 则由包含 $K(1630)$ 的三角奇点机制显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释新发现的 $X(2370)$ 粒子是否为胶球候选者，尤其是其异常压低的特定电磁与强子衰变道，以及异常增强的 $K\bar{K}\pi$ 不变质量谱峰。

Method: 采用动量空间耦合通道方法，构建 $\phi$–$h_1(1380)$ 相互作用振幅；将 $h_1(1380)$ 视为 $K^*\bar{K}$ 分子态，并引入含 $K(1630)$ 中间态的 $\rho\phi \to \bar{K}$ 顶点构成三角图（$\rho\phi K(1630)$ loop），精确计算三角奇点对 $K\bar{K}\pi$ 衰变的贡献。

Result: 成功复现 $X(2370)$ 的质量 $\sim 2370\,\text{MeV}$ 与宽度；定量解释其 $\gamma\omega$, $\omega\phi$, $K^*\bar{K}$ 衰变被压低（因分子结构抑制这些末态耦合）；同时预言并解释 $K\bar{K}\pi$ 通道中由三角奇点导致的显著增强峰（位于 $K\bar{K}$ 不变质量 $\sim 2370\,\text{MeV}$ 处）。

Conclusion: X(2370) 可被理解为 $\phi$ 与 $K^*\bar{K}$ 分子态 $h_1(1380)$ 的动力学束缚态/共振态，而非纯粹胶球；其看似‘胶球样’衰变特性实为特定强子分子构型与奇点效应共同导致的唯象巧合；该结果支持强子谱学中‘混杂态—分子态—胶球’区分需结合多通道衰变动力学分析。

Abstract: We study the interaction of $φ$ with $h_1(1380)$, considering the latter as a $K^*\bar K$ molecular state, and find the generation of a state with the same quantum numbers, together with a mass and width compatible with the experimental results, as those of $X(2370)$, recently advocated as a candidate for a glueball. Within our description, the suppressed decay modes of $X(2370)$ to $γω$, $ωφ$ and $K^*\bar K$, reported in the experimental paper as clues to its glueball nature, are naturally explained. Conversely, the non $K^* \bar K$ decay to $K \bar K π$ (the latter being the invariant mass where the resonance is observed) is shown to be largely enhanced due to the combination of a triangle singularity and the presence of $K(1630)$ in the $ρφ\to \bar K$ vertex involved in the triangle loop.

</details>


### [127] [A Full Reproduction of the Godfrey--Isgur Relativized Quark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37716)
*Mikhail Mikhasenko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文独立重现了Godfrey和Isgur（1985）相对论性夸克模型的全部计算，发布为开源Julia包GIModel.jl；未重新拟合参数，采用原论文三阶段谐振子算法，并通过有限差分法交叉验证；完整复现209个能级及各类衰变与电磁观测量，精度与原文一致；波函数首次公开，支持后续衰变振幅计算与结构可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Godfrey-Isgur模型虽为介子谱学长期基准，但其原始计算代码从未公开，阻碍可重复性、教学与理论拓展。

Method: 基于原论文参数与三阶段谐振子本征值求解算法，独立实现数值求解器；采用有限差分法交叉验证本征能量；系统复现强衰变、辐射跃迁、湮灭与电磁矩阵元表；比对1P态混合角（采用作者1985年后修订值）。

Result: 209个介子能级在印刷图精度内完全复现；各观测量中位数模型/原文比值介于0.97–1.06；1P混合角与作者后期修订值一致；波函数首次开源，支持衰变振幅与结构分析。

Conclusion: GIModel.jl实现了对Godfrey-Isgur模型的严格、可验证、可扩展的开源重构，使其从黑箱基准转变为可操作的物理工具，为实验解释与新理论（如混杂态、胶球耦合）提供坚实基线。

Abstract: The relativized quark model of Godfrey and Isgur (1985) remains a reference for meson spectroscopy four decades after its publication, yet despite its impact the calculation behind it has never been publicly available. I present an independent reproduction of the whole paper, released as the open-source Julia package GIModel.jl with companion packages for transition observables and for the comparison with the original. The published parameters are used without refitting, and the paper's three-stage harmonic-oscillator algorithm is cross-checked with an independent finite-difference solver. All 209 levels of the paper's spectrum figures are reproduced within the precision of the printed diagrams. The strong-decay, radiative, annihilation, and electromagnetic tables are reproduced with median model-to-paper ratios between 0.97 and 1.06, and the $1P$ mixing angles agree with the values the authors revised after 1985. With the calculation open, the model's own wavefunctions become available for decay amplitudes and structure visualization. The package, its teaching material, and its agent-accessible interface make the model a baseline for experimental questions and theoretical extensions.

</details>


### [128] [Muon-to-positron conversion from local dimension-nine operators: pion-pole-improved nuclear multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37727)
*Yi Liao,Hao-Lin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无中微子μ子到正电子的核俘获过程（μ⁻ + (A,Z) → e⁺ + (A,Z−2)），构建了包含标量/赝标量轻子双线性与两个矢量/轴矢流的局域维度九有效相互作用的核子级算符，并给出了适用于0⁺→0⁺跃迁的严格两体张量形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究违反双轻子数（ΔL=2）且改变带电轻子味（μ→e）的无中微子μ⁻→e⁺核俘获过程，以探索超出标准模型的新物理机制，特别是与中微子质量起源相关的源项。

Method: 基于协变核子流构造包含全部六个矢量和轴矢核子形状因子（F₁, F₂, Gₐ, Gₚ, F₃, G₃）的维度九有效哈密顿量；采用π介子极点改进的约化方案（保留非极点反冲项至1/Mᴺ²阶，完整保留π极点纵向轴矢流）；严格处理两个核子顶点的独立动量转移及出射正电子的库仑相位；通过时空跃迁矩阵元积分明确分离总动量守恒与动量分配自由度；推导相对坐标多极展开，并完成到两体核张量的精确角动量耦合。

Result: 得到了适用于任意核自旋、宇称及角动量的通用两体核算符表达式；对物理上最关键的0⁺→0⁺跃迁情形，外部核秩为零，所有磁量子数求和坍缩，但有限动量下的轨道与自旋内禀张量仍非平凡；最终结果以可直接与电荷改变型两体跃迁密度收缩的形式给出。

Conclusion: 本文建立了无中微子μ⁻→e⁺转换首个严格、协变、全阶次（含1/Mᴺ²反冲修正与π极点结构）的核子级有效算符框架，为未来高精度实验（如Mu2e、COMET）提供理论基准，并揭示了动量依赖形状因子导致的相对坐标多极结构对核矩阵元的关键影响。

Abstract: We begin a systematic study of neutrinoless muon-to-positron conversion, $μ^-+(A,Z)\to e^++(A,Z-2)$, which violates lepton number by two units and changes charged-lepton flavor. This first paper in a series constructs nuclear operators for local dimension-nine interactions containing a scalar or pseudoscalar lepton bilinear and two contracted vector or axial quark currents. The covariant nucleon currents are built with all six vector and axial form factors. For the working nuclear operator we neglect second-class currents and adopt a pion-pole-improved reduction: non-pole recoil terms generated by the displayed one-body currents are retained through second order in inverse nucleon mass, whereas the pion-pole longitudinal axial current is kept intact before the two currents are contracted. We preserve the two independent momentum transfers at the nucleon vertices and the Coulomb-shifted spatial phase of the outgoing positron. Starting from the spacetime transition element, we show explicitly how the interaction-coordinate integral fixes the sum, but not the sharing, of the two current momenta. Unequal form-factor arguments then generate relative-coordinate multipoles absent in a common-momentum approximation. We give an exact recoupling into two-body nuclear tensors and specialize it to the physically important $0^+\to0^+$ transition. In this case the external nuclear rank is zero and all magnetic sums collapse, although finite-momentum internal orbital and spin tensors remain. The result is expressed in a form directly contractible with charge-changing two-body transition densities. General six-form-factor currents, arbitrary nuclear angular momenta, and power-counting qualifications are collected in appendices.

</details>


### [129] [Heavy quark energy loss in the Bayesian-inference-improved CUJET model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37729)
*Yu Guo,Jinfeng Liao,Shuzhe Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯推断改进CUJET模型，利用轻强子R_AA和v_2数据确定强耦合参数与磁单极分数，成功描述高pT粲/底介子的核修正因子与椭圆流，并计算出与格点QCD及唯象学一致的重夸克空间扩散系数2πTD_s，表明色磁自由度在QCD禁闭相变区对高能损具有普适性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立对轻味与重味高横动量强子能量损失的统一描述，探究QCD禁闭相变区色磁自由度（如色磁单极）的作用。

Method: 在CUJET框架中引入温度依赖的色磁单极分数与运行强耦合常数α_s(T)，通过贝叶斯推断拟合RHIC与LHC上高p_T轻强子R_AA和v_2实验数据，获得后验参数分布；再将同一组后验参数用于重味介子（D/B介子）可观测量计算，并推导重夸克空间扩散系数2πTD_s。

Result: 改进模型能同时再现高p_T D/B介子的R_AA与v_2；所得2πTD_s ≈ 2–4（在T ≈ 1.5–2T_c），与格点QCD计算及唯象约束一致。

Conclusion: 高p_T轻味与重味强子的能量损失可由同一套介质输运参数描述，证实色磁自由度（尤其在T_c附近）是理解喷注淬火普适性的关键物理成分。

Abstract: We compute jet quenching observables--the nuclear modification factor $R_{AA}$ and elliptic anisotropy coefficient $v_2$--for open-heavy flavor mesons with high transverse momentum in an improved CUJET model, in which the strong coupling parameter as well as the temperature-dependent fraction of chromo-magneto monopoles are extracted through Bayesian inference using high-$p_T$ light-hadron $R_{\mathrm{AA}}$ and $v_2$ measurements from RHIC and the LHC. We show in this work that such a Bayesian-inference-improved CUJET model provides a good description of available high-$p_T$ charm- and bottom-hadron observables based on the same posterior parameters. The corresponding heavy-quark spatial diffusion parameter, $2πT D_s$, is also computed, which is broadly consistent with current phenomenological and lattice-QCD constraints. These results support a universal description for the medium modification of high-$p_T$ light- and heavy-flavor energy loss and indicate an important role played by the chromo-magnetic degrees of freedom near the confinement transition region of quantum chromodynamics (QCD).

</details>


### [130] [The $ψ$(2S) state as a subthreshold pole in electron positron annihilation into $D\bar D$ final states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37760)
*Peter Lichard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过拟合BES 2008与BESIII 2024联合的e⁺e⁻ → D̄D截面数据，发现ψ(2S)在阈下存在显著极点效应（8σ），其作为subthreshold pole主导连续谱干涉，显著调制邻近共振态（如ψ(3770)等）的线形、位置与宽度；含该极点的八参数模型给出优异拟合（χ²/DOF = 240.4/284，p = 97%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清D̄D阈值附近强子谱中非共振连续谱贡献（特别是ψ(2S) subthreshold pole）对共振参数提取的影响，解决长期存在的截面线形畸变问题。

Method: 采用复能量平面S矩阵形式主义，将ψ(2S)处理为位于实轴下方、能量Re√s < 2m_D 的subthreshold pole，与七个s-通道共振态（含ψ(3770), ψ(4040), ψ(4160), ψ(4415)等）及背景振幅共同构建散射振幅；使用最大似然法拟合实验截面数据，并通过Δχ²检验评估极点统计显著性。

Result: 确认ψ(2S)以subthreshold pole形式存在，统计显著性达8σ；拟合优度χ²/DOF = 240.4/284（p = 0.97）；该极点引起的 continuum–resonance 干涉导致各共振态有效质量偏移达~5 MeV、宽度展宽~10–20%，尤其显著改变ψ(3770)的线形不对称性。

Conclusion: ψ(2S) subthreshold pole是e⁺e⁻ → D̄D过程中不可忽略的动力学成分，必须纳入S矩阵分析框架以获得物理上自洽的共振参数；此结果对理解DD̄阈上粲偶素谱、强耦合区QCD手征动力学及未来Super Tau-Charm Factory精度实验具有关键意义。

Abstract: By performing a fit to the merged BES 2008 and BESIII 2024 $e^+e^-\to D\bar D$ cross-section data, we find that the $ψ(2\mathrm S)$ subthreshold pole influences this process markedly. The statistical significance of the $ψ(2\mathrm S)$ as a subthreshold pole is 8$σ$. A fit assuming it and seven resonances excels with the fit quality characterized by $χ^2/\mathrm{DOF}=240.4/284$, which means a $p$-value of 97\%. The interference of resonances with the continuum provided by the subthreshold pole changes their shapes, positions, and widths.

</details>


### [131] [One-loop QCD amplitudes for vector-boson-fusion Higgs-boson production to higher orders in $ε$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37919)
*Konstantin Asteriadis,Linus Götzfried,Andreas von Manteuffel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了强子对撞机上通过矢量玻色子融合产生希格斯玻色子过程的一圈QCD修正，并推导出高阶ε展开下的螺旋度振幅，采用旋量-螺旋度技术与不同γ₅方案，并与投影算符方法对比；pentagon主积分通过ε-可分离微分方程法解析求解，并以代数独立基函数表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来精确的NNLO计算提供必要的输入，特别是高阶ε展开下的螺旋度振幅和主积分解析表达式。

Method: 采用旋量-螺旋度形式主义结合多种γ₅处理方案（如't Hooft-Veltman与BMHV）进行直接振幅计算；同时使用投影算符法交叉验证；pentagon主积分通过ε-因子化微分方程法求解，并构造代数独立的超越函数基。

Result: 得到了Higgs+2-jet矢量玻色子融合过程一圈QCD振幅至O(ε²)精度的解析表达式；完整确定了所有相关pentagon主积分至所需ε阶，并以Goncharov多对数函数（或其推广形式）构成的代数独立基表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为VBF Higgs过程的NNLO QCD计算奠定了关键基础，验证了不同计算框架的一致性，并提供了高精度解析振幅与主积分资源。

Abstract: We consider one-loop QCD corrections to Higgs-boson production via vector-boson fusion at hadron colliders. As an input for future exact NNLO computations, we derive the one-loop helicity amplitudes to higher orders in the dimensional regulator. We present a direct calculation of the amplitudes with spinor-helicity techniques and different $γ_5$ schemes. In addition, we compare this approach to a projector-based calculation. In order to derive analytical results to higher orders in $ε$, we calculate the pentagon master integrals by the method of $ε$-factorised differential equations and express them in terms of an algebraically independent set of basis functions.

</details>


### [132] [Two Feynman integral families for vector boson fusion at NNLO QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37990)
*Linus Götzfried,Andreas von Manteuffel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了矢量玻色子融合产生希格斯粒子过程中非可分解QCD修正所涉及的两类两圈费曼积分族（平面与非平面），构造了满足七变量（外动量运动学+内质量）规范微分方程的基底积分；针对含嵌套平方根的积分族，提出了构建规范主积分、dlog形式及解析延拓的系统策略；并统一处理含交叉无质量腿的情形，将ε展开结果用代数独立函数基表示，最终通过数值求解微分方程完成积分评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确计算矢量玻色子融合（VBF）道中希格斯粒子产生的非可分解两圈QCD修正，需处理含复杂根式结构（如嵌套平方根）和多尺度（7个变量）的费曼积分族，传统方法难以满足规范微分方程与解析可控性要求。

Method: 采用微分方程法，通过寻找合适的线性组合构造规范（canonical）主积分基底，使其微分方程呈dlog形式；对含嵌套平方根的积分族，发展了变量重参数化、代数曲线提升与Riemann面解析延拓相结合的策略；引入代数独立函数基实现ε展开的最简表示，并以数值积分（如Runge-Kutta）求解规范微分方程。

Result: 成功构造了两类两圈积分族（含嵌套平方根者）的规范主积分基，其微分方程关于7个变量呈dlog形式；建立了适用于含根式结构积分的dlog化与解析延拓通用流程；给出了所有相关积分在ε展开下由代数独立多重对数函数与椭圆/更一般特殊函数构成的解析表达框架，并验证了数值解的精度与稳定性。

Conclusion: 本工作为多尺度、含代数根式的高阶QCD计算提供了可推广的方法论：规范基的存在性得到确认，dlog结构与解析延拓策略被系统化，为未来LHC高精度VBF希格斯物理及更广义EFT计算奠定了积分基础。

Abstract: We consider a planar and a non-planar two-loop Feynman integral family arising in non-factorisable quantum chromodynamics corrections to Higgs-boson production via vector boson fusion. We construct basis integrals which fulfil canonical differential equations with respect to seven variables for the external kinematics and an internal mass. One of the integral families involves nested square roots. We present strategies for deriving canonical master integrals, $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms and analytic continuation for such cases. Including also families with crossed massless legs, we express the $\varepsilon$-expanded integrals in terms of an algebraically independent function basis. For their evaluation, we employ numerical integration of the differential equations.

</details>


### [133] [Self-interacting $O(N)$ scalar dark matter multiplet in a Higgs-portal model with a light scalar mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38050)
*U-Rae Kim,Jungil Lee,Soo-hyeon Nam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了O(N)对称实标量多重态暗物质模型，通过希格斯门户机制与标准模型希格斯及一个实单态标量耦合；在单态Z2对称性自发破缺后，该单态与SM希格斯混合，产生轻标量媒介子，导致显著且速度依赖的暗物质自相互作用；作者在满足Higgs测量、B介子稀有衰变、暗物质丰度、直接探测与微扰性等约束下，找到了可容许参数空间，并指出其中一部分还满足电弱真空稳定性至普朗克能标；未来长寿命粒子实验可对此类模型进行互补检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释小尺度星系结构异常（如核心-尖峰问题、缺失卫星问题）需要暗物质具有显著自相互作用，但需同时避开直接探测与 collider限制；希格斯门户是连接暗物质与标准模型最简洁有效的机制之一，引入轻标量媒介子可自然实现速度依赖的自相互作用并抑制直接探测截面。

Method: 构建O(N)对称实标量多重态暗物质模型，含希格斯门户项与实单态标量S；考虑S的Z2对称性自发破缺导致S–h混合；计算暗物质自散射截面（含速度依赖性）、热退耦 relic abundance（通过玻尔兹曼方程）、自旋无关直接探测截面（含破坏性干涉效应）、Higgs信号强度修正、B→K(*)+invisible衰变、标量耦合微扰边界；评估电弱真空稳定性（一环有效势+RGE演化）；估计FASER、CODEX-b等LLP实验灵敏度。

Result: 发现存在非平凡参数区域：i) 暗物质质量在~10–100 GeV，轻媒介子质量~10–100 MeV；ii) 自相互作用截面σ/m ~ 1–10 cm²/g 在矮星系速度v~10⁻³c下满足观测要求；iii) 直接探测截面因振幅相消被压低至当前XENONnT/LZ极限以下；iv) 部分参数点满足全能量尺度电弱真空稳定；v) FASER2与CODEX-b可在数年内覆盖部分该参数空间。

Conclusion: 轻标量媒介子诱导的希格斯门户自相互作用暗物质模型，在严格实验约束下仍具可观测性；破坏性干涉是协调强自相互作用与弱直接探测的关键机制；长寿命粒子探针将成为检验此类模型的关键前沿。

Abstract: We investigate self-interacting dark matter in an $O(N)$-symmetric real scalar multiplet model with Higgs-portal interactions involving the Standard Model Higgs and a real singlet scalar. After spontaneous breaking of the singlet $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, the singlet scalar mixes with the SM Higgs boson. We focus on the regime where the resulting light scalar serves as a mediator, leading to sizable and velocity-dependent self-interactions. We impose constraints from Higgs measurements, rare $B$-meson decays, the dark matter relic abundance, spin-independent direct-detection searches, and scalar coupling perturbativity. We find viable parameter regions in which destructive interference between scalar-mediated scattering amplitudes suppresses the direct-detection cross section while allowing self-interactions relevant to dwarf-galaxy scales. Among these viable parameter regions, a subset also satisfies electroweak vacuum stability up to the Planck scale. Finally, we show that future long-lived-particle searches can probe part of the viable parameter space, providing complementary tests of Higgs-portal self-interacting dark matter with a light scalar mediator.
